<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">m) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> start</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> before.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> m[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">].</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // Slash commands only at the start of the message.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (char </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "/"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> before.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, start).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">trim</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> ""</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { char, query: m[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">], start, end: caret }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> score</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">query</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> q</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> query.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toLowerCase</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">q) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (base.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">startsWith</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(q)) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 4</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (t.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">includes</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(q)) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // Subsequence match.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> q.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  /** Shows the dictate button; without it there is none. */</span></span>
